    22	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#10 — a covered total unique key reads one row; additional named equalities check that row.
    23	    pub(super) fn read_value(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, mode: ReadMode, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Expr> {
    24	        let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
    25	        for key in &read.keys { if let Some(field) = &key.column { super::super::row_views::predicate_field(info, field, &read.site)?; } }
    26	        if !read.seeks.is_empty() { return Err(type_error(&read.site, "a seek needs a bounded page", "t[prefix, col >= start] first 50 by index")); }
    27	        let mut point = read.clone();
    28	        if point.keys.is_empty() && info.maintained.as_ref().is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()) {
    29	            point.keys.push(ast::KeyPart { column: None, value: A::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(true), site: read.site.clone() }, site: read.site.clone() });
    30	            point.index = Some("byGroup".into());
    31	        } else if point.keys.is_empty() {
    32	            return Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{}[] reads nothing; a point read needs a key", read.table), format!("{}[id]", read.table)));
    33	        }
    34	        let implicit_group = mode == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.maintained.is_some() && point.index.is_none();
    35	        if implicit_group { point.index = Some("byGroup".into()); }
    36	        let (mut keys, index, mut selection) = self.select_index(info, &point, mode, out)?;
    37	        super::super::point_key::check(info, &index, read, &keys.iter().map(|(column, _, ty)|
    38	            (column.as_deref(), !ty.is_optional() && *ty != Ty::Unknown)).collect::<Vec<_>>(), false)?;
    39	        if implicit_group { selection = "complete maintained group key".into(); }
    40	        let extra = keys.len() > index.columns.len();
    41	        let mut equalities = Vec::new();
    42	        if extra {
    43	            for (column, value, ty) in &mut keys {
    44	                let column = column.as_ref().expect("named key");
    45	                if column != "id" && !info.columns.contains_key(column) {
    46	                    return Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no column {column}", read.table), name_rewrite(column, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)));
    47	                }
    48	                let expected = self.key_ty(info, &index, column);
    49	                if !ty.comparable(&expected) { return Err(type_error(&read.site, format!("{column} is {}; {}.{column} is {}", ty.name(), read.table, expected.name()), format!("use {} for {column}", expected.name()))); }
    50	                *value = self.snapshot(value.clone(), out);
    51	                if !index.columns.contains(column) { equalities.push((column.clone(), value.clone())); }
    52	            }
    53	            keys.retain(|(column, _, _)| index.columns.contains(column.as_ref().unwrap()));
    54	        }
    55	        let key = self.order_keys(info, &index, keys)?;
    56	        let location = self.site();
    57	        self.record_selection(&location, read, &index, if mode == ReadMode::Exists { "exists" } else if info.maintained.is_some() { "scalar" } else { "row or null" }, &selection);
    58	        self.sources.insert(location.clone(), (read.site.clone(), print::read_expr(read)));
    59	        let mut point = Read { site: location, table: read.table.clone(), index: index.name, key, provenance: KeyProvenance::Shape };
    60	        // @ref LLP 3000.028#3.4 — only a caller-proved existence read may use the whole owner partition.
    61	        if mode == ReadMode::Exists && equalities.is_empty() {
    62	            super::super::own_keys::existence(self.schema, &mut point);
    63	        }
    64	        if equalities.is_empty() { return Ok(if mode == ReadMode::Exists { Expr::Exists(point) } else { Expr::Get(point) }); }
    65	        let row = self.temp("probe");
    66	        out.push(Stmt::Let { name: row.clone(), value: Expr::Get(point) });
    67	        let mut condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::var(&row)), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) };
    68	        // @ref LLP 3000.014#5-what-derives-from-the-capture — a denied aggregate carries a null value, not a present group.
    69	        if mode == ReadMode::Exists && info.maintained.is_some() {
    70	            let present = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::field(ir::var(&row), "value")), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) };
    71	            condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left: Box::new(condition), right: Box::new(present) };
    72	        }
    73	        for (column, value) in equalities {
    74	            let test = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left: Box::new(ir::field(ir::var(&row), &column)), right: Box::new(value) };
    75	            condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left: Box::new(condition), right: Box::new(test) };
    76	        }
    77	        if mode == ReadMode::Exists { return Ok(condition); }
    78	        out.push(Stmt::If { condition, then_body: Vec::new(), else_body: vec![Stmt::Assign { name: row.clone(), value: Expr::Literal(Value::Null) }] });
    79	        Ok(ir::var(&row))
    80	    }

    82	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.3 — a seek constrains the first index column after the pinned prefix.
    83	    pub(super) fn seek(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<ir::Range>> {
    84	        if read.seeks.is_empty() { return Ok(None); }
    85	        let mut range = ir::Range { gt: None, gte: None, lt: None, lte: None };
    86	        for bound in &read.seeks {
    87	            if index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) != Some(&bound.column) || read.matching.is_some() {
    88	                return Err(type_error(&bound.site, "a seek compares the first unpinned column of a plain index", "t[prefix, col >= start, col < end] first 50 by index"));
    89	            }
    90	            let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, &bound.column);
    91	            let (value, ty) = self.field_value(&ObjectField { spread: false, name: bound.column.clone(), value: Some(bound.value.clone()), site: bound.site.clone() }, &expected, out)?;
    92	            if !ty.comparable(&expected) || ty.is_optional() {
    93	                let endpoint = print::expression(&bound.value);
    94	                let rewrite = if ty.is_optional() {
    95	                    format!("put the read inside `if {endpoint} != null:`, then `{} where .{} {} {endpoint}`", read.table, bound.column, bound.op)
    96	                } else {
    97	                    format!("use a present {} endpoint in `{} where .{} {} {endpoint}`", expected.required().name(), read.table, bound.column, bound.op)
    98	                };
    99	                return Err(type_error(&bound.site, format!("seek on {}.{} expects present {}, got {}", read.table, bound.column, expected.required().name(), ty.name()), rewrite));
   100	            }
   101	            let slot = match bound.op.as_str() { ">" => &mut range.gt, ">=" => &mut range.gte, "<" => &mut range.lt, "<=" => &mut range.lte, _ => unreachable!("parser seek comparison") };
   102	            if slot.is_some() { return Err(type_error(&bound.site, "a seek bound is repeated", "write one lower and one upper bound")); }
   103	            *slot = Some(Box::new(self.snapshot(value, out)));
   104	        }
   105	        if (range.gt.is_some() && range.gte.is_some()) || (range.lt.is_some() && range.lte.is_some()) {
   106	            return Err(type_error(&read.site, "a seek has at most one lower and one upper bound", "t[prefix, col >= start, col < end] first 50 by index"));
   107	        }
   108	        // Null sorts below every present value. An upper-only nullable seek
   109	        // must skip the entire null segment, including its id suffixes.
   110	        if range.gt.is_none() && range.gte.is_none()
   111	            && read.seeks.first().is_some_and(|bound| self.key_ty(info, index, &bound.column).is_optional()) {
   112	            range.gt = Some(Box::new(ir::lit(Value::Null)));
   113	        }
   114	        Ok(Some(range))
   115	    }
   116	}

   118	impl Binder<'_> {
   119	    pub(super) fn variant(&self) -> Option<ir::VariantId> {
   120	        (!self.variants.is_empty()).then(|| {
   121	            let hash = snapback4_core::value::digest(&self.variants);
   122	            std::array::from_fn(|i| u32::from_str_radix(&hash[i * 8..i * 8 + 8], 16).expect("hex digest"))
   123	        })
   124	    }

   126	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.5 — a declared page shape after a scalar predicate needs no wrapper or correction loop.
   127	    pub(super) fn page_shape(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<A> {
   128	        let predicate = read.filter.as_ref()?;
   129	        let (base, name, site) = trailing_shape(predicate)?;
   130	        self.catalog.shapes.get(name)?;
   131	        // Type the operand in isolation: no speculative setup, notes or source sites
   132	        // escape. A valid row/object shape inside the predicate keeps its meaning.
   133	        let mut probe = self.clone();
   134	        probe.focal = Some(("$filter".into(), Ty::Row(read.table.clone())));
   135	        probe.kind = ProgramKind::Query;
   136	        probe.in_predicate = true;
   137	        let (_, ty) = probe.expr(base, &mut setup::Body::default()).ok()?;
   138	        if matches!(ty.required(), Ty::Row(_) | Ty::Object(_) | Ty::RowShape(_) | Ty::RowObject(_) | Ty::Page(_, _) | Ty::Collection(_, _) | Ty::List(_, _)) { return None; }
   139	        let mut page = read.clone();
   140	        remove_trailing_shape(page.filter.as_mut()?);
   141	        Some(A::ShapeUse { base: Box::new(A::Read(page)), name: name.into(), site: site.clone() })
   142	    }

   144	    pub(super) fn scan_filter(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl) -> Fallible<(ReadExpr, Option<Box<Expr>>)> {
   145	        let mut read = read.clone();
   146	        let Some(mut predicate) = read.filter.take() else { return Ok((read, None)); };
   147	        // Planning knows parameter declarations; binding also knows helper,
   148	        // local and lane selectors. Only present, infallible endpoints may
   149	        // leave the short-circuit predicate in either pass.
   150	        let required = self.scopes.iter().flat_map(|scope| scope.keys())
   151	            .filter(|name| self.binding(name).is_some_and(|binding| !binding.ty.is_optional()))
   152	            .cloned().collect();
   153	        if !read.order_keys.is_empty() && read.matching.is_none() {
   154	            let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
   155	            let mut parts = Vec::new(); super::super::intent::split(*predicate, &mut parts);
   156	            let mut residual = Vec::new();
   157	            for part in parts {
   158	                let candidate = super::super::intent::comparison(&part).map(|(column, op, value)| (column, vec![op], value))
   159	                    .or_else(|| super::super::intent::equality(&part, info)
   160	                        .filter(|(column, _)| !self.key_ty(info, index, column).is_optional())
   161	                        .map(|(column, value)| (column, vec![">=".into(), "<=".into()], value)));
   162	                if let Some((column, ops, value)) = candidate
   163	                    && index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) == Some(&column)
   164	                    && super::super::intent_aggregates::safe_endpoint(&value, &required)
   165	                    && ops.iter().all(|op| !read.seeks.iter().any(|bound| bound.op.starts_with('<') == op.starts_with('<'))) {
   166	                    for op in ops { read.seeks.push(ast::SeekBound { column: column.clone(), op, value: value.clone(), site: part.site().clone() }); }
   167	                } else { residual.push(part); }
   168	            }
   169	            let Some(remaining) = super::super::intent::conjunction(residual) else { return Ok((read, None)); };
   170	            predicate = remaining;
   171	        }
   172	        // A single next-column comparison is the same seek as its key spelling.
   173	        if read.seeks.is_empty() && read.matching.is_none()
   174	            && let A::Binary { op, left, right, site } = predicate.as_ref()
   175	            && matches!(op.as_str(), ">" | ">=" | "<" | "<=")
   176	            && let A::Member { base, name, .. } = left.as_ref()
   177	            && matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. })
   178	            && index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) == Some(name)
   179	            && !has_focal(right)
   180	            && super::super::intent_aggregates::safe_endpoint(right, &required)
   181	        {
   182	            read.seeks.push(ast::SeekBound { column: name.clone(), op: op.clone(), value: *right.clone(), site: site.clone() });
   183	            self.notes.push(Diagnostic::new(site, "N_SEEK", "a `where` on the next index column is a seek; write it in the key", print::read_expr(&read)).guide("reads"));
   184	            return Ok((read, None));
   185	        }
   186	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.3 — an unscoped filter can spend the whole page meter on unrelated authors.
   187	        if self.kind == ProgramKind::Query && read.keys.is_empty() && read.seeks.is_empty() && read.matching.is_none()
   188	            && crate::bind::unique_bound::group_bound(self.schema, &read.table, &[]).is_none_or(|n| n > 128)
   189	            && has_focal(&predicate) && !pins_leading_column(&predicate, index.columns.first().map(String::as_str))
   190	        {
   191	            let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
   192	            let follows = follow_edge_filter(&predicate, info);
   193	            let consequence = if follows {
   194	                "pages come back short and incomplete when followed authors are sparse (`complete: false`)"
   195	            } else { "sparse matches can exhaust the row meter and return short `complete: false` pages" };
   196	            // @ref LLP 3000.036#12 — wave 2 (024): a `where` every row passes is the trivial repair.
   197	            let rewrite = match always_true(&predicate, info) {
   198	                Some(column) => format!("drop this `where`: .{column} is required, so every row passes it"),
   199	                None if follows => "pin a column with `where .col = value`, choose `by .col`, or read a `view` (see `snapback4 guide feeds`)".to_owned(),
   200	                None => "pin a column with `where .col = value`, choose `by .col`, read a `view` for a count or total, or drop a `where` every row passes".to_owned(),
   201	            };
   202	            self.notes.push(Diagnostic::new(predicate.site(), "N_SPARSE_FILTER",
   203	                format!("{} by {}: this unscoped `where` pins no index prefix; {consequence}", read.table, index.name),
   204	                rewrite).guide(if follows { "feeds" } else { "reads" }));
   205	        }
   206	        let value = self.row_predicate(&read.table, &predicate)?;
   207	        Ok((read, Some(Box::new(value))))
   208	    }
   209	
   210	    pub(super) fn row_predicate(&mut self, table: &str, predicate: &A) -> Fallible<Expr> {
   211	        let row_ty = Ty::Row(table.into());
   212	        let prior = self.focal.replace(("$filter".into(), row_ty.clone()));
   213	        let kind = std::mem::replace(&mut self.kind, ProgramKind::Query);
   214	        let predicate_context = std::mem::replace(&mut self.in_predicate, true);
   215	        let mut body = setup::Body::default();
   216	        self.push_scope();
   217	        // A fresh lexical row snapshot lets short-circuit guards narrow its
   218	        // fields without leaking facts to another scan or an enclosing row.
   219	        self.scopes.last_mut().unwrap().insert("$filter".into(), Binding {
   220	            runtime: "$filter".into(), declared: row_ty.clone(), ty: row_ty,
   221	            parameter: false, revision: 0, fields: BTreeMap::new(), error: None, origins: Vec::new(),
   222	        });
   223	        let bound = self.expr(predicate, &mut body);
   224	        self.pop_scope();
   225	        self.kind = kind;
   226	        self.in_predicate = predicate_context;
   227	        self.focal = prior;
   228	        let (value, ty) = bound?;
   229	        if !ty.is_bool() { return Err(type_error(predicate.site(), format!("where needs a bool predicate, got {}", ty.name()), "where .col = value")); }
   230	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.6 — predicate setup, including lazy reads, runs afresh for each rule-visible row.

   297	    pub(super) fn name(&mut self, name: &str, site: &Site) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   298	        match name {
   299	            "viewer" | "$input:viewer" => Ok((Expr::Input(Input::Viewer), Ty::Principal)),
   300	            "now" | "commit" => {
   301	                if self.kind == ProgramKind::Query && name == "commit" {
   302	                    return Err(unknown(site, "commit is the mutation commit clock; queries read now", "now"));
   303	                }
   304	                Ok((Expr::Input(Input::Now), Ty::Time))
   305	            }
   306	            "new" => {
   307	                if self.viewer_depth.is_some() { return Err(job_reads::read_only(site)); }
   308	                if self.kind == ProgramKind::Query {
   309	                    return Err(unknown(site, "queries cannot mint ids", "mint ids in a mutation"));
   310	                }
   311	                Ok((Expr::Input(Input::NewId), Ty::Id))
   312	            }
   313	            "true" => Ok((Expr::Literal(Value::Bool(true)), Ty::Bool)),
   314	            "false" => Ok((Expr::Literal(Value::Bool(false)), Ty::Bool)),
   315	            "null" => Ok((Expr::Literal(Value::Null), Ty::Null)),
   316	            _ => {
   317	                if let Some(binding) = self.binding(name) {
   318	                    if let Some(error) = &binding.error { return Err(error.clone()); }
   319	                    return Ok((ir::var(&binding.runtime), binding.ty.clone()));
   320	                }
   321	                if self.catalog.tables.contains_key(name) {
   322	                    return Err(unknown(site, format!("{name} is a table; a read needs a key or a range"), format!("{name}[key]  -- or: {name}[prefix] first 50 by index")));
   323	                }
   324	                let declared: Vec<&str> = self.scopes.iter().flat_map(|s| s.keys().map(String::as_str)).chain(["viewer", "now", "new"]).collect();
   325	                Err(unknown(site, format!("unknown name {name}"), name_rewrite(name, declared, None)))
   326	            }
   327	        }
   328	    }

   330	    /// The type of `expr` without binding it into this program.
   331	    fn speculative_type(&self, expr: &A) -> Option<Ty> {
   332	        let mut probe = self.clone();
   333	        probe.computation(expr, &mut setup::Body::default()).ok().map(|(_, ty)| ty)
   334	    }

   336	    /// A local name for the row a reference column names, unused in scope.
   337	    fn row_local(&self, column: &str) -> String {
   338	        let taken = |name: &str| self.scopes.iter().any(|scope| scope.contains_key(name)) || self.catalog.tables.contains_key(name);
   339	        let mut name = column.to_owned();
   340	        if taken(&name) { name.push_str("Row"); }
   341	        while taken(&name) { name.push('_'); }
   342	        name
   343	    }

   345	    /// `x.a.b.c` where `x.a` is a required reference: read the `a` row, then one hop.
   346	    fn second_hop_rewrite(&self, base: &A, target: &str, field: &str) -> Option<String> {
   347	        let A::Member { base: inner, name: hop, .. } = base else { return None };
   348	        let Some(Ty::Ref(first)) = self.speculative_type(inner).filter(|ty| !ty.is_optional()) else { return None };
   349	        let reference = print::expression(inner);
   350	        let row = self.row_local(inner_name(inner).unwrap_or(&first));

   354	    }

   357	    fn present_row_rewrite(&self, operand: &A, op: &str, left: &A, right: &A) -> Option<String> {

     5	    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.2 — literal columns retain their encoding and seek;

     7	    pub(super) fn extremum(&mut self, name: &str, args: &[A], out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<(Expr, Ty)>> {
     8	        if matches!(&args[1], A::Member { base, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. })) {
     9	            return self.column_extremum(name, args, out);
    10	        }

    12	            self.catalog.extremum_domains.get(&args[0].site().to_string()).filter(|read|

    72	    // @ref LLP 3000.013#5.1 — domains retain aggregate admission and coverage.
    73	    fn column_extremum(&mut self, name: &str, args: &[A], out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<(Expr, Ty)>> {
    74	        let A::Member { base, name: field, .. } = &args[1] else { return Ok(None); };
    75	        if !matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. }) { return Ok(None); }

    77	        // binary call such as min(value, .column) inside an existing shape.

    80	            self.is_page(&read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint)?.then_some(read)
    81	        } else { None };

    83	            Ok(value) => Some(value),
    84	            Err(error) if domain.is_none() => return Err(error),
    85	            // A streaming domain need not be a valid materialized page.
    86	            Err(_) => None,
    87	        };
    88	        if let Some((_, ty)) = probed {
    89	            let Some((item, _)) = ty.sequence() else { return Ok(None); };

    91	                && !other.is_optional() && ty.comparable(&other) {

    93	                    return Err(ambiguous(args[1].site(), format!("{name} can compare two collections or aggregate .{field} from the first"),
    94	                        format!("bind `other = .{field}` before this call and write `{name}({}, other)` for the binary comparison; for an aggregate, call `{name}(rows, .{field})` outside the enclosing shape", print::expression(&args[0]))).guide("reads"));
    95	                }
    96	                return Ok(None);
    97	            }
    98	        }
    99	        let nonempty = domain.as_ref().is_some_and(|read| self.aggregate_nonempty(read));
   100	        let (source, item, domain) = if let Some(mut read) = domain {
   101	            // Bare table names reach the binder without intent planning.
   102	            if matches!(&args[0], A::Name { .. }) {
   103	                let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
   104	                if let Some(index) = info.indexes.iter().filter(|index| index.is_plain() && index.columns.first() == Some(field)).min_by_key(|index| &index.name) {
   105	                    read.index = Some(index.name.clone());
   106	                }
   107	            }
   108	            let (mut scan, _) = self.scan_at(&read, out, prefix::Position::Aggregate)?;
   109	            if scan.orders_field(self.schema, field) {
   110	                scan.order = if name == "max" { Order::Desc } else { Order::Asc };
   111	                // Nulls contribute no extremum. Seek past their entire index
   112	                // segment, including when there are no present values.
   113	                if self.schema.tables[&read.table].columns.get(field).is_some_and(|column| matches!(column, snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Optional(_))) {
   114	                    let range = scan.range.get_or_insert(ir::Range { gt: None, gte: None, lt: None, lte: None });

   119	            // candidate limit for advice only; the aggregate IR keeps its domain.
   120	            // Expired rows are charged and skipped before a value qualifies.
   121	            let mut advice = scan.clone();

   127	            (source, ty.sequence().expect("probed sequence").0, false)
   128	        };

   130	        // Column ordering uses the same total order as binary min/max. Nulls
   131	        // contribute no value, including when every row's column is null.
   132	        let result = if nonempty && !ty.is_optional() { ty } else { ty.optional() };

   134	    }
   135	}

    55	    // @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — no predicted delta enters receipt-derived state.
    56	    pub(super) fn defer_receipt_maintain(&self, m: &Maintain) -> bool {

    59	
    60	    // Cache ordinary group keys once per target/query. Predicates and receipt
    61	    // fields are never evaluated; unknown/computed keys conservatively freeze
    62	    // the target. An unpinned read is incomplete if any group is dirty, while
    63	    // each returned row still decides independently whether to recount.
    64	    #[inline(never)]
    65	    pub(super) fn receipt_maintain_pending(&mut self, m: &Maintain, group: Option<&[Value]>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    66	        if !self.defer_receipt_maintain(m) { return Ok(false); }
    67	        if !self.receipt_pending_groups.contains_key(&m.target) {
    68	            let fields = if let Some(k) = &m.row_key { vec![k.source_key.clone()] }
    69	                else { m.by.iter().map(|f| m.aliases.get(f).unwrap_or(f).clone()).collect() };

    71	            if let Some(k) = &m.row_key {
    72	                let member = self.receipt_overlay_groups(&k.table, std::slice::from_ref(&k.member_key), false)?;
    73	                groups = groups.zip(member).map(|(mut source, member)| { source.extend(member); source });
    74	            }

    78	            group.map_or(!keys.is_empty(), |group| keys.iter().any(|key| key == group)));

    86	    fn receipt_overlay_groups(&self, table: &str, fields: &[String], computed: bool) -> Result<Option<Vec<Vec<Value>>>, RuntimeError> {
    87	        if !self.tx.has_provisional(table)? { return Ok(Some(Vec::new())); }

    94	    // Recover a read's parent/group from the pinned index. A member point uses

    96	    pub(super) fn receipt_read_group(&mut self, m: &Maintain, index: &str, key: &[Value]) -> Result<Option<Vec<Value>>, RuntimeError> {
    97	        if !self.defer_receipt_maintain(m) { return Ok(None); }

   111	    pub(super) fn maintain(&mut self, source: &str, old: Option<&Value>, new: Option<&Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   112	        self.receipt_pending_groups.clear();

   114	        // predicate cannot select that work on a device, so use the same public
   115	        // worst-case reservation as update/delete before skipping it. Already

   118	            m.source == source && m.private.is_none() && m.row_key.is_none() && (self.schema.receipt_dependencies.contains_key(&m.target) || self.schema.tables[&m.target].sync.is_none())) {

   120	        }

   138	    pub(super) fn maintain_scratch<T>(&mut self, bytes: u64, work: impl FnOnce(&mut Self) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
   139	        if self.maintain_prepaid { return work(self); }

   146	    fn maintain_one(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, old: Option<&Value>, new: Option<&Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   147	        let table = self.schema.table(&maintain.source).expect("validated");
   148	        let mut changes: Vec<(Vec<Value>, i128)> = Vec::new();
   149	        for (image, direction) in [(old, -1), (new, 1)] {
   150	            let Some(image) = image else { continue };

   152	            let image = crate::schema::row_image(image, table);
   153	            if !maintain.selected(&image) { continue; }

   155	            let change = delta(maintain, &image, direction)?;
   156	            if let Some(existing) = changes.iter_mut().find(|(k, _)| *k == key) { existing.1 += change; }
   157	            else { changes.push((key, change)); }
   158	        }
   159	        for (key, change) in changes {
   160	            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   161	            if let Some(retiring) = self.retiring.as_mut() {
   162	                let entry = retiring.aggregates.entry((maintain.target.clone(), canonical_json(&key))).or_insert_with(|| (key, 0));

   164	                continue;
   165	            }
   166	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {
   167	                self.apply_aggregate(maintain, key, change, None)?;
   168	            }
   169	        }
   170	        Ok(())
   171	    }

   173	    /// Apply a retirement's net aggregate changes, one per target row, in

   175	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   176	    pub(super) fn apply_retired_aggregates(&mut self, changes: BTreeMap<(String, String), (Vec<Value>, i128)>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   177	        let schema = self.schema;

   180	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {

   188	    pub(crate) fn rebuild_maintain_reported(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, report: &mut crate::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {

   197	    /// Derive every legacy-to-digest aggregate id map to a fixed point, then

   200	    /// Every pass is inside the adoption writer transaction, so no reader can
   201	    /// observe a dangling intermediate reference.
   202	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   203	    pub(crate) fn rewrite_legacy_maintained_references(&mut self, maintains: &[Maintain]) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
   204	        let mut legacy = Vec::new();
   205	        for maintain in maintains.iter().filter(|maintain| maintain.private.is_none() && maintain.row_key.is_none()) {
   206	            let target = self.schema.tables[&maintain.target].clone();
   207	            let fields: Vec<_> = maintain.group_fields().into_iter().map(|field| {

   209	                (field, kind)

   231	            for (target, rows) in &legacy {
   232	                let target_ids = next_ids.entry(target.clone()).or_default();
   233	                for (old, key) in rows {

   250	        if !settled {
   251	            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("legacy aggregate identity references form a cycle".into()));
   252	        }
   253	
   254	        let references: Vec<_> = self.schema.tables.iter().filter_map(|(name, table)| {
   255	            let columns: Vec<_> = table.columns.iter().filter_map(|(column, kind)|
   256	                has_legacy_maintained_reference(kind, &ids).then(|| (column.clone(), kind.clone())))
   257	                .collect();
   258	            (!columns.is_empty()).then(|| (name.clone(), table.clone(), columns))
   259	        }).collect();
   260	        let mut rewritten = 0_u64;
   261	        for (name, table, columns) in references {
   262	            let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
   263	            loop {
   264	                let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &name, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   265	                if page.is_empty() { break; }
   266	                for (old, encoded) in page {
   267	                    bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&encoded);
   268	                    let mut next = old.clone();
   269	                    let mut changed = false;
   270	                    if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut next {
   271	                        for (column, kind) in &columns {
   272	                            let Some(value) = fields.get_mut(column) else { continue };
   273	                            changed |= rewrite_legacy_maintained_reference(kind, value, &ids);
   274	                        }
   275	                    }
   276	                    if changed {
   277	                        self.tx.put(&name, &table, &next, Some(&old))?;
   278	                        rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
   279	                    }
   280	                }
   281	            }
   282	        }
   283	        Ok(rewritten)

   286	    /// Build one aggregate from the rows in the store: the target's rows go,
   287	    /// then every source row contributes once. A schema change that adds or
   288	    /// redefines a `maintain` calls this, so a count over existing rows is
   289	    /// right from its first read (day two, LLP 2000.000 §11.2).
   290	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   291	    pub(crate) fn rebuild_maintain(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   292	        let heap = self.meter.used.heap_bytes;
   293	        let saved = self.column_rebuild;
   294	        self.column_rebuild |= maintain.private.is_some();
   295	        let result = self.rebuild_maintain_inner(maintain);
   296	        self.column_rebuild = saved;
   297	        self.meter.used.heap_bytes = heap;
   298	        result
   299	    }
   300	
   301	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   302	    fn rebuild_maintain_inner(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   303	        if maintain.row_key.is_some() { return self.rebuild_row_view(maintain); }
   304	        let target = self.schema.table(&maintain.target).expect("validated");
   305	        let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
   306	        let target_bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(target).saturating_mul(16 + 4 * target.indexes.len() as u64);
   307	        self.meter.heap(target_bytes.saturating_mul(256))?;
   308	        loop {
   309	            let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &maintain.target, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   310	            if page.is_empty() { break; }
   311	            for (existing, key) in page {
   312	                bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key);
   313	                self.refuse_timeline_write(&maintain.target, crate::schema::Effect::Delete)?;
   314	                let entries = self.tx.delete(&maintain.target, target, &existing)?;
   315	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
   316	                self.row_changed(&maintain.target, Some(&existing), None, Origin::Maintained)?;
   317	            }
   318	        }
   319	        let source = self.schema.table(&maintain.source).expect("validated");
   320	        self.meter.heap(super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(source).saturating_mul(256))?;
   321	        let mut totals: BTreeMap<String, (Vec<Value>, i128)> = BTreeMap::new();
   322	        bounds = Bounds::default();
   323	        loop {
   324	            let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &maintain.source, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   325	            if page.is_empty() { break; }
   326	            for (mut image, key) in page {
   327	                self.rebuilt_source_rows += 1;
   328	                bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key);
   329	                materialize_optional_fields(&mut image, source);
   330	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   331	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Steps, maintain.evaluation_steps())?;
   332	                if !maintain.selected(&image) { continue; }
   333	                // @ref LLP 3000.028#6 — a total unique owner key has one contribution; backfill streams bounded pages instead of retaining all lifetime keys in a totals map.
   334	                if maintain.own_keys.is_some() {
   335	                    let heap = self.meter.used.heap_bytes;
   336	                    self.meter.heap(target_bytes)?;
   337	                    self.apply_aggregate(maintain, maintain.source_key(&image), 1, Some(true))?;
   338	                    self.meter.used.heap_bytes = heap;
   339	                    continue;
   340	                }
   341	                self.meter.heap(target_bytes)?;
   342	                let key = maintain.source_key(&image);
   343	                let change = delta(maintain, &image, 1)?;
   344	                let entry = match totals.entry(canonical_json(&key)) {
   345	                    std::collections::btree_map::Entry::Occupied(entry) => {
   346	                        self.meter.used.heap_bytes -= target_bytes;
   347	                        entry.into_mut()
   348	                    }
   349	                    std::collections::btree_map::Entry::Vacant(entry) => entry.insert((key, 0)),
   350	                };

   362	    fn live_sum(&self, maintain: &Maintain) -> bool {
   363	        matches!(maintain.kind, MaintainKind::Sum { .. }) && self.schema.table(&maintain.source).is_some_and(|source| source.is_expiring())
   364	    }
   365	
   366	    /// A zero sum is still a group while source rows remain: one signed
   367	    /// contribution may expire before the other. Keep its candidate row so
   368	    /// a scan, snapshot and offline replica can discover the later live sum.
   369	    fn aggregate_source_present(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: &[Value]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   370	        // One returned source image and its encoded key (MemoryRows also materializes count_prefix).
   371	        let bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[&maintain.source]).saturating_mul(2);
   372	        self.maintain_scratch(bytes, |runner| {
   373	            let source = &runner.schema.tables[&maintain.source];
   374	            let index = maintain.contribution_index();
   375	            if !source.indexes.contains_key(&index) { return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!("maintain {} needs its contribution index", maintain.target))); }
   376	            runner.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   377	            let present = runner.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.count_prefix(allowance, &maintain.source, &index, key, 1))? != 0;
   378	            // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9 — a local positive witness keeps zero; absence needs complete, currently held source coverage.
   379	            if !present && runner.device && !runner.aggregate_source_complete(maintain, key)? {
   380	                runner.unknown_fact(&maintain.source, &index, key, "zero-sum occupancy");
   381	                return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("the zero-sum group's source occupancy is not known locally; queued without a predicted row".into()));
   382	            }
   383	            Ok(present)
   384	        })
   385	    }
   386	
   387	    fn aggregate_source_complete(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: &[Value]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   388	        let source = &self.schema.tables[&maintain.source];
   389	        if source.sync.as_ref().is_none_or(|sync| sync.horizon.is_some()) || self.schema.maintain_subset_unknown(&maintain.target) { return Ok(false); }
   390	        // The target has the same bare audience keys, but no source horizon.
   391	        if !self.upsert_absence_proven(&maintain.target, "byGroup", &target_group_key(maintain, key))? { return Ok(false); }
   392	        for (name, index) in &source.indexes {
   393	            let values = index.columns().iter().map_while(|column| maintain.by.iter().position(|field| field == column).map(|n| key[n].clone())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   394	            if self.retained_group(&maintain.source, name, &values)? { return Ok(false); }
   395	        }
   396	        Ok(true)
   397	    }

   399	    fn apply_aggregate(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: Vec<Value>, change: i128, source_present: Option<bool>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   400	        if self.schema.timelines.iter().any(|t| t.owns(&maintain.target)) {
   401	            return Err(super::timeline::refusal("E_TIMELINE_OWNED", "timeline-owned tables cannot be aggregate targets; maintain a mapped application table instead"));
   402	        }
   403	        let id = maintained_id(&key);
   404	        let target = self.schema.table(&maintain.target).expect("validated");
   405	        self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   406	        let existing = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, &maintain.target, &id))?;
   407	        let target_key = target_group_key(maintain, &key);
   408	        if self.device && existing.is_none() && !self.upsert_absence_proven(&maintain.target, "byGroup", &target_key)? {
   409	            self.unknown_fact(&maintain.target, "byGroup", &target_key, "aggregate base");
   410	            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("the affected aggregate value or its absence is not known locally; queued without a predicted row".into()));
   411	        }
   412	        let old_value = existing.as_ref().and_then(|row| row.field("value").ok()).map(Value::as_int).transpose()?.unwrap_or(0);
   413	        let next = i128::from(old_value) + change;
   414	        if !(I53_MIN as i128..=I53_MAX as i128).contains(&next) {
   415	            return Err(aggregate_overflow(maintain));
   416	        }
   417	        let keep_zero = next == 0 && self.live_sum(maintain) && match source_present {
   418	            Some(present) => present,
   419	            None => self.aggregate_source_present(maintain, &key)?,
   420	        };
   421	        if next == 0 && !keep_zero {
   422	            if let Some(existing) = existing {
   423	                self.refuse_timeline_write(&maintain.target, crate::schema::Effect::Delete)?;
   424	                let entries = self.tx.delete(&maintain.target, target, &existing)?;
   425	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
   426	                self.row_changed(&maintain.target, Some(&existing), None, Origin::Maintained)?;
   427	            }
   428	            self.mirror_column(maintain, &key, 0)?;
   429	            return Ok(());
   430	        }
   431	        let mut row = crate::small_map::collect([("id".to_owned(), Value::Id(id.clone())), ("value".to_owned(), Value::Int(next as i64))]);
   432	        for (field, value) in maintain.group_fields().iter().zip(key.iter().cloned()) {
   433	            row.insert(field.clone(), value);
   434	        }
   435	        // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#3-global-maintained-view-representation — stores index the one logical empty group by a hidden constant.
   436	        if maintain.by.is_empty() { row.insert(GLOBAL.into(), Value::Bool(true)); }
   437	        let row = Value::Row(row.into());
   438	        if existing.as_ref() == Some(&row) { return Ok(()); }
   439	        let entries = self.tx.put(&maintain.target, target, &row, existing.as_ref())?;
   440	        self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
   441	        self.row_changed(&maintain.target, existing.as_ref(), Some(&row), Origin::Maintained)?;
   442	        self.mirror_column(maintain, &key, next as i64)
   443	    }
   444	}

   446	pub(super) fn delta(maintain: &Maintain, source: &Value, direction: i64) -> Result<i128, RuntimeError> {
   447	    Ok(match &maintain.kind {
   448	        MaintainKind::Count => i128::from(direction),
   449	        MaintainKind::Sum { of } => i128::from(direction).checked_mul(i128::from(source.field(of)?.as_int()?)).ok_or_else(|| aggregate_overflow(maintain))?,
   450	    })
   451	}

   453	// @ref LLP 3000.007#17 — an internal sum names its maintain and column, never a retained contribution or computed value.
   454	pub(super) fn aggregate_overflow(maintain: &Maintain) -> RuntimeError {
   455	    RuntimeError::AggregateOverflow { maintain: maintain.private.as_ref().map_or_else(|| maintain.target.clone(), |o|o.table.clone()), column: maintain.private.as_ref().map_or_else(|| "value".into(), |o|o.column.clone()) }
   456	}

   459	mod timeline_tests {
   460	    use super::*;
   461	    #[test]
   462	    fn timeline_owned_tables_cannot_be_maintain_targets() {
   463	        let schema = crate::engine::timeline_authority::write_tests::schema();
   464	        let mut store = crate::Store::memory().unwrap(); store.install(&schema).unwrap();
   465	        let tx = store.transaction().unwrap();
   466	        let mut runner = Runner::new(&tx, &schema, crate::Context::system().at(1), crate::meter::Meter::new(crate::Ceilings::unbounded(),crate::meter::Cut::Abort),0);
   467	        let mut guard = runner.timeline_scope(crate::engine::timeline_authority::Authority::Transition("feed".into()));
   468	        for suffix in crate::schema::timeline::OWNED_SUFFIXES {
   469	            let maintain = Maintain { own_keys: None, row_key: None, parent_key: None, aliases: Default::default(), target: format!("feed{suffix}"), source: "follows".into(), by: vec!["readerId".into()],
   470	                kind: MaintainKind::Count, filter: None, computed: Default::default(), residency: Default::default(), readable: None, private: None };
   471	            let result = guard.apply_aggregate(&maintain, vec![Value::Id("reader".into())], 1, None);
   472	            assert_eq!(result.unwrap_err().code(), "E_TIMELINE_OWNED");
   473	        }
   474	    }
   475	}

     7	pub(super) const AUTHORIZATION: &str = "$maintainAuthorization";

    10	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
    11	enum AuthorizationDomain {
    12	    /// Program `exists` observes only rows readable by the current viewer.
    13	    Program,
    14	    /// A table rule's witness tests raw row presence below the target rule.
    15	    Rule,
    16	}
    17	
    18	impl Binder<'_> {
    19	    /// A private maintained point is nullable: authorization may withhold a
    20	    /// stored value and the logical zero alike. Keep the numeric type required
    21	    /// only when the key or a surviving guard proves the view's read rule.
    22	    // @ref LLP 3000.013#14.7 — hidden maintained hits and misses are null;
    23	    // an admitted miss alone becomes the aggregate identity.
    24	    pub(super) fn maintained_result_type(
    25	        &self,
    26	        maintain: &ast::MaintainDecl,
    27	        read: &ReadExpr,
    28	        ty: Ty,
    29	    ) -> Ty {
    30	        if !self.maintained_parent_is_live(maintain, read) {
    31	            return ty.optional();
    32	        }
    33	        if (self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job && self.viewer_depth.is_none())
    34	            || self
    35	                .schema
    36	                .table(&read.table)
    37	                .is_some_and(|table| table.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable())
    38	        {
    39	            return ty;
    40	        }
    41	        let Some(expected) = substituted_rule(maintain, read) else {
    42	            return ty.optional();
    43	        };
    44	        if self.authorization_proved(&expected, true) {
    45	            ty
    46	        } else {
    47	            ty.optional()
    48	        }
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    /// A system job bypasses read policy, but an inherited maintained point
    52	    /// still disappears with its through parent. A dominating exact-point
    53	    /// witness or the live parent row supplying `.id` proves that lifetime.
    54	    fn maintained_parent_is_live(&self, maintain: &ast::MaintainDecl, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
    55	        let Some(parent) = self.schema.maintain_for(&read.table).and_then(|maintain| maintain.parent_key.as_ref()) else { return true };
    56	        let Some(key) = group_keys(maintain, read).remove(&parent.reference) else { return false };
    57	        let site = key.site().clone();
    58	        let mut parent_read = ReadExpr {
    59	            cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), order_keys: Vec::new(), table: parent.parent.clone(),
    60	            keys: vec![ast::KeyPart { column: None, value: key, site: site.clone() }], seeks: Vec::new(), filter: None,
    61	            index: None, range: None, matching: None, site,
    62	        };
    63	        if self.point_present(&parent_read) { return true; }
    64	        parent_read.keys[0].column = Some("id".into());
    65	        self.point_present(&parent_read)
    66	    }
    67	
    68	    /// Store a truth fact on every lexical root it depends on. Existing branch
    69	    /// intersection and rebinding logic then keeps exactly the facts that
    70	    /// survive control flow and clears facts whose operands change.
    71	    pub(super) fn record_maintain_authorization(&mut self, condition: &A, truth: bool) {
    72	        let Some((key, roots)) = self.authorization_key(condition, truth, AuthorizationDomain::Program) else {
    73	            return;
    74	        };
    75	        for root in roots {
    76	            let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&root).cloned() else {
    77	                return;
    78	            };
    79	            binding.fields.insert(key.clone(), Ty::Bool);
    80	            self.scopes
    81	                .last_mut()
    82	                .expect("a scope")
    83	                .insert(root, binding);
    84	        }
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    fn authorization_holds(&self, condition: &A, truth: bool) -> bool {
    88	        self.authorization_key(condition, truth, AuthorizationDomain::Rule)
    89	            .is_some_and(|(key, roots)| {
    90	                roots.iter().all(|root| {
    91	                    self.binding(root)
    92	                        .is_some_and(|binding| binding.fields.get(&key) == Some(&Ty::Bool))
    93	                })
    94	            })
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    /// Facts may prove a read rule without restating the whole expression:
    98	    /// one admitted disjunct proves an `or`, while every conjunct proves an
    99	    /// `and`. This is the same propositional narrowing used by control flow.
   100	    pub(super) fn authorization_proved(&self, condition: &A, truth: bool) -> bool {
   101	        if self.condition_known(condition, truth) || self.authorization_holds(condition, truth) {
   102	            return true;
   103	        }
   104	        match condition {
   105	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => self.authorization_proved(value, !truth),
   106	            A::Binary {
   107	                op, left, right, ..
   108	            } if op == "and" && truth => {
   109	                self.authorization_proved(left, true) && self.authorization_proved(right, true)
   110	            }
   111	            A::Binary {
   112	                op, left, right, ..
   113	            } if op == "and" => {
   114	                self.authorization_proved(left, false) || self.authorization_proved(right, false)
   115	            }
   116	            A::Binary {
   117	                op, left, right, ..
   118	            } if op == "or" && truth => {
   119	                self.authorization_proved(left, true) || self.authorization_proved(right, true)
   120	            }
   121	            A::Binary {
   122	                op, left, right, ..
   123	            } if op == "or" => {
   124	                self.authorization_proved(left, false) && self.authorization_proved(right, false)
   125	            }
   126	            _ => false,
   127	        }
   128	    }
   129	
   130	    fn authorization_key(
   131	        &self,
   132	        condition: &A,
   133	        truth: bool,
   134	        domain: AuthorizationDomain,
   135	    ) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
   136	        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
   137	        let key = self.condition_identity(condition, truth, domain, &mut roots)?;
   138	        if roots.is_empty() {
   139	            roots.insert(pure_calls::CONSTANTS.to_owned());
   140	        }
   141	        Some((vec![AUTHORIZATION.into(), key], roots))
   142	    }
   143	
   144	    fn condition_identity(
   145	        &self,
   146	        condition: &A,
   147	        truth: bool,
   148	        domain: AuthorizationDomain,
   149	        roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>,
   150	    ) -> Option<String> {
   151	        match condition {
   152	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => {
   153	                self.condition_identity(value, !truth, domain, roots)
   154	            }
   155	            A::Exists { read, .. } => {
   156	                let raw = matches!(domain, AuthorizationDomain::Rule)
   157	                    || truth
   158	                    || self.program_absence_is_raw(read);
   159	                let identity = self.read_identity("exists", if raw { "raw" } else { "readable" }, read, roots)?;
   160	                Some(format!("truth:{truth}:{identity}"))
   161	            }
   162	            A::Created { read, .. } => {
   163	                let identity = self.read_identity("created", "raw", read, roots)?;
   164	                Some(format!("truth:{truth}:{identity}"))
   165	            }
   166	            A::Binary {
   167	                op, left, right, ..
   168	            } if matches!(op.as_str(), "=" | "!=") => {
   169	                let mut operands = [
   170	                    self.value_identity(left, domain, roots)?,
   171	                    self.value_identity(right, domain, roots)?,
   172	                ];
   173	                operands.sort();
   174	                let equal = (op == "=") == truth;
   175	                Some(format!("compare:{equal}:{:?}", operands))
   176	            }
   177	            _ => Some(format!(
   178	                "truth:{truth}:{}",
   179	                self.value_identity(condition, domain, roots)?
   180	            )),
   181	        }
   182	    }
   183	
   184	    fn value_identity(&self, value: &A, domain: AuthorizationDomain, roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<String> {
   185	        match value {
   186	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } => {
   187	                Some(format!("unary:{op}:{}", self.value_identity(value, domain, roots)?))
   188	            }
   189	            A::Binary {
   190	                op, left, right, ..
   191	            } => Some(format!(
   192	                "binary:{op}:{}:{}",
   193	                self.value_identity(left, domain, roots)?,
   194	                self.value_identity(right, domain, roots)?
   195	            )),

    37	// cursors, or authorized result positions. Storage images cannot call a raw
    38	// scalar/key pricing API; row fields use AuthorizedRow::scalar_heap instead.
    39	struct DisclosedKey<'a>(&'a Value);
    40	struct DisclosedKeys<'a>(&'a [Value]);
    41	struct DisclosedBounds<'a>(&'a Bounds);
    42	impl DisclosedKey<'_> {
    43	    fn heap(self) -> u64 {
    44	        match self.0 {
    45	            Value::String(s) | Value::Id(s) => word_heap::text(s),
    46	            Value::Bytes(b) => word_heap::allocation(b.len()),
    47	            Value::Array(a) => DisclosedKeys(a).heap(), _ => 0,
    48	        }
    49	    }
    50	    fn copy_heap(self) -> u64 {
    51	        match self.0 {
    52	            Value::String(s) | Value::Id(s) => s.len() as u64,
    53	            Value::Bytes(b) => b.len() as u64,
    54	            Value::Array(a) => {
    55	                word_heap::slots::<Value>(a.len()) + a.iter().map(|v|DisclosedKey(v).copy_heap()).sum::<u64>()
    56	            }
    57	            _ => 0,
    58	        }
    59	    }
    60	}
    61	impl DisclosedKeys<'_> {
    62	    fn heap(self) -> u64 { word_heap::slots::<Value>(self.0.len()) + self.0.iter().map(|v|DisclosedKey(v).heap()).sum::<u64>() }
    63	}
    64	impl DisclosedBounds<'_> {
    65	    fn heap(self) -> u64 {
    66	        let b = self.0;
    67	        [&b.after, &b.at_or_after, &b.before].into_iter().flatten().map(|v|DisclosedKeys(v).heap()).sum::<u64>()
    68	            + [&b.gt, &b.gte, &b.lt, &b.lte].into_iter().flatten().map(|v|DisclosedKey(v).heap()).sum::<u64>()
    69	            + b.word.as_ref().map_or(0, |s| word_heap::text(s))
    70	    }
    71	}

    73	fn key_verdict_heap(index: &crate::schema::Index, prefix: &[Value], viewer: &str, rule: &crate::schema::RuleExpr) -> u64 {
    74	    // The prefix row's collect buffer, map, Arc, viewer and probe term clones.
    75	    let fields = index.components.iter().zip(prefix).filter_map(|(c,v)| c.column().map(|name| (name, v)));
    76	    let row = fields.map(|(name,v)| {
    77	            word_heap::node::<String, Value>() + word_heap::text(name) + DisclosedKey(v).heap()
    78	        }).sum::<u64>();
    79	    word_heap::vec_growth::<(String, Value)>(prefix.len()) + row + word_heap::slots::<usize>(2) + word_heap::text(viewer) + rule_values(rule, prefix, viewer)
    80	}
    81	fn rule_values(rule: &crate::schema::RuleExpr, prefix: &[Value], viewer: &str) -> u64 {
    82	    use crate::schema::{RuleExpr::*, RuleTerm};
    83	    let term = |t: &RuleTerm| match t {
    84	        RuleTerm::Viewer => word_heap::text(viewer), RuleTerm::Literal(v) => DisclosedKey(v).heap(),
    85	        RuleTerm::Reference { .. } | RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { .. } | RuleTerm::Field(_) | RuleTerm::OldField(_) | RuleTerm::NextField(_) => prefix.iter().map(|v|DisclosedKey(v).heap()).sum(),
    86	    };
    87	    match rule {
    88	        Eq(a,b) | Ne(a,b) => term(a) + term(b),
    89	        And(rs) | Or(rs) => rs.iter().map(|r| rule_values(r, prefix, viewer)).sum(), Not(r) => rule_values(r, prefix, viewer),
    90	        Exists { table, key, equalities, .. } | Created { table, key, equalities, .. } => {
    91	            word_heap::text(table)
    92	            + word_heap::vec_growth::<Value>(key.len()) + key.iter().map(term).sum::<u64>()
    93	            + word_heap::vec_growth::<(String, Value)>(equalities.len()) + equalities.iter().map(|(name,v)| word_heap::text(name)+term(v)).sum::<u64>()
    94	        }
    95	        _ => 0,
    96	    }
    97	}

    99	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — PAGE admits discovery and target-lane metadata before allocation.
   100	/// Temporary `(table, index)` images built while retaining a rule's complete
   101	/// dependency envelope. Count them without allocating so PAGE lanes can admit
   102	/// the buffer before `rules::probe_sites` clones any names into it.
   103	fn rule_probe_sites_heap(rule: &crate::schema::RuleExpr) -> u64 {
   104	    use crate::schema::{RuleExpr::*, RuleTerm};
   105	    fn term(term: &RuleTerm) -> (usize, u64) {
   106	        term.references().fold((0usize, 0u64), |(count, bytes), (table, _)| {
   107	            (count.saturating_add(1), bytes.saturating_add(word_heap::text(table) + word_heap::text("byId")),
   108	                )
   109	        })
   110	    }
   111	    fn walk(rule: &crate::schema::RuleExpr) -> (usize, u64) {
   112	        let add = |(ac, ab): (usize, u64), (bc, bb): (usize, u64)| {
   113	            (ac.saturating_add(bc), ab.saturating_add(bb))
   114	        };
   115	        match rule {
   116	            Eq(a, b) | Ne(a, b) | Compare(_, a, b) => add(term(a), term(b)),
   117	            Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } | Created { table, index, key, equalities, .. } => key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_, term)| term)).fold(
   118	                    (1, word_heap::text(table) + word_heap::text(index)),
   119	                    |sum, item| add(sum, term(item)),
   120	                ),
   121	            And(rules) | Or(rules) => rules.iter().fold((0, 0), |sum, rule| add(sum, walk(rule))),
   122	            Not(rule) => walk(rule),
   123	            Allow | Deny | Public { .. } => (0, 0),
   124	        }
   125	    }
   126	    let (count, names) = walk(rule);
   127	    word_heap::vec_growth::<(String, String)>(count).saturating_add(names)
   128	}

   130	fn metered_dependency_table(
   131	    meter: &mut crate::meter::Meter,
   132	    read_tables: &mut BTreeSet<String>,
   133	    table: &str,
   134	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   135	    if !read_tables.contains(table) {
   136	        meter.heap(word_heap::text(table)
   137	            + word_heap::insertion::<String, ()>(read_tables.len()))?;
   138	        read_tables.insert(table.to_owned());
   139	    }
   140	    Ok(())
   141	}

   160	struct MergeHead {
   161	    key: Vec<Value>,
   162	    row: Value,
   163	    lane: usize,
   164	    order: Order,
   165	}
   166	
   167	impl Ord for MergeHead {
   168	    fn cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Ordering {
   169	        let order = self.key.iter().zip(&other.key).find_map(|(a, b)| {
   170	            let c = a.total_cmp(b);
   171	            (c != Ordering::Equal).then_some(c)
   172	        }).unwrap_or_else(|| self.key.len().cmp(&other.key.len()));
   173	        let order = match self.order { Order::Asc => order.reverse(), Order::Desc => order };
   174	        order.then_with(|| other.lane.cmp(&self.lane))
   175	    }
   176	}
   177	impl PartialOrd for MergeHead { fn partial_cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Option<Ordering> { Some(self.cmp(other)) } }
   178	impl PartialEq for MergeHead { fn eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool { self.cmp(other) == Ordering::Equal } }
   179	impl Eq for MergeHead {}

     6	// @ref LLP 3000.013#5.1 — choose an access path, leaving aggregate scan

     8	#[derive(Clone, Default)]
     9	pub(super) struct Contexts {
    10	    pub domains: BTreeSet<String>,
    11	    pub extrema: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    12	    required: BTreeMap<String, BTreeSet<String>>,
    13	}
    14	impl Contexts {
    15	    pub fn safe(&self, read: &ReadExpr, value: &Expr) -> bool {
    16	        safe_endpoint(value, self.required.get(&read.site.to_string()).unwrap_or(&BTreeSet::new()))
    17	    }
    18	    fn visit(&mut self, value: &mut Expr, tables: &BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>, functions: &BTreeMap<String, FunctionDecl>, required: &BTreeSet<String>) {
    19	        if let Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Exists { read, .. } = value {
    20	            self.required.insert(read.site.to_string(), required.clone());
    21	        }
    22	        if let Expr::Call { name, args, .. } = value
    23	            && is_consumer(name, args, tables) && !functions.contains_key(name)
    24	            && let Some(Expr::Read(read)) = args.first() && read.range.is_none() {
    25	            self.domains.insert(read.site.to_string());
    26	            if matches!(name.as_str(), "min" | "max") && let Some(field) = args.get(1).and_then(intent::column) {
    27	                self.extrema.insert(read.site.to_string(), field.into());
    28	            }
    29	        }
    30	        consumer(value, tables, functions, required);
    31	    }
    32	}
    33	pub(super) fn order(catalog: &mut Catalog) -> Contexts {
    34	    let mut contexts = Contexts::default();
    35	    for doc in &mut catalog.documents {
    36	        for program in &mut doc.programs {
    37	            let required = required_args(&program.args);
    38	            let mut visit = |value: &mut Expr| contexts.visit(value, &catalog.tables, &catalog.functions, &required);
    39	            intent_walk::statements(&mut program.prelude, &mut visit);
    40	            intent_walk::statements(&mut program.body, &mut visit);
    41	        }
    42	        for function in &mut doc.functions {
    43	            let required = required_args(&function.args);
    44	            let mut visit = |value: &mut Expr| contexts.visit(value, &catalog.tables, &catalog.functions, &required);
    45	            match &mut function.body {
    46	                FunctionBody::Expression(value) => intent_walk::expression(value, &mut visit),
    47	                FunctionBody::Block(body) => intent_walk::statements(body, &mut visit),
    48	            }
    49	        }
    50	        for shape in &mut doc.shapes {
    51	            intent_walk::shape(&mut shape.fields, &mut |value| contexts.visit(value, &catalog.tables, &catalog.functions, &BTreeSet::new()));
    52	        }
    53	    }
    54	    contexts
    55	}

    57	// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.2 — defer overload selection to the typed binder.
    58	// Optional filters and inferred lanes may materialize an ordinary read before
    59	// then. Keep the unpaged domain as an alternative for a scalar projection;
    60	// a binary collection comparison still receives its original page plan.
    61	pub(super) fn remember_domains(catalog: &mut Catalog) {
    62	    for doc in &mut catalog.documents {
    63	        intent_walk::document(doc, &mut |expr| {
    64	            if let Expr::Call { name, args, .. } = expr
    65	                && matches!(name.as_str(), "min" | "max") && args.len() == 2
    66	                && !catalog.functions.contains_key(name) && intent::column(&args[1]).is_none()
    67	                && let Expr::Read(read) = &args[0]
    68	                && read.range.is_none() && read.matching.is_none()
    69	                && catalog.tables.get(&read.table).is_some_and(|table|
    70	                    !table.maintained.as_ref().is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty())) {
    71	                catalog.extremum_domains.insert(read.site.to_string(), read.clone());
    72	            }
    73	        });
    74	    }
    75	}

    77	fn required_args(args: &[ArgDecl]) -> BTreeSet<String> {
    78	    args.iter().filter(|arg| !arg.type_.optional).map(|arg| arg.name.clone()).collect()
    79	}
    80	
    81	pub(super) fn is_consumer(name: &str, args: &[Expr], tables: &BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>) -> bool {
    82	    if matches!(name, "count" | "sum") { return true; }
    83	    if !matches!(name, "min" | "max") { return false; }
    84	    let field = args.get(1).and_then(intent::column);
    85	    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.2 — expression extrema use ordinary aggregate scan planning.
    86	    // Collection-valued second arguments keep binary page comparison planning.
    87	    if field.is_none() && args.get(1).is_none_or(may_be_collection) { return false; }
    88	    // A binary comparison of two pages keeps their observable row order.
    89	    // A focal member absent from this table can belong to an outer shape.
    90	    match args.first() {
    91	        Some(Expr::Read(read)) => tables.get(&read.table).is_some_and(|table|
    92	            extremum_domain(read, table) && field.is_none_or(|field| field == "id" || table.columns.contains_key(field))),
    93	        _ => true,
    94	    }
    95	}
    96	
    97	// Before type binding, only select an aggregate order for a definitely scalar
    98	// result. Otherwise leave the source order intact for the binary overload;
    99	// the binder can still admit a scalar aggregate over that ordinary scan.
   100	fn may_be_collection(value: &Expr) -> bool {
   101	    match value {
   102	        Expr::Literal { .. } | Expr::Unary { .. } | Expr::Exists { .. } | Expr::Created { .. } => false,
   103	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "??" || op == "+" => may_be_collection(left) || may_be_collection(right),
   104	        Expr::Binary { .. } => false,
   105	        Expr::If { then, otherwise, .. } => may_be_collection(then) || may_be_collection(otherwise),
   106	        Expr::Assert { value, .. } => may_be_collection(value),
   107	        // Calls can be user helpers, including helpers which return pages.
   108	        _ => true,
   109	    }
   110	}
   111	
   112	fn extremum_domain(read: &ReadExpr, table: &TableInfo) -> bool {
   113	    if !read.order_keys.is_empty() || read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some()
   114	        || read.filter.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty() || read.keys.is_empty() { return true; }
   115	    // Decide the argument's kind before adding an aggregate order. In
   116	    // particular, a complete maintained group lookup is a scalar total.
   117	    // Positional keys without an index retain their point/refusal path;
   118	    // only the binder can resolve their types or diagnose an ambiguity.
   119	    let named = read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_some());
   120	    if !named && read.index.is_none() { return false; }
   121	    let index = read.index.as_deref().or_else(|| table.maintained.as_ref().map(|_| "byGroup"));
   122	    !table.indexes.iter().any(|candidate| candidate.unique && candidate.is_plain()
   123	        && index.is_none_or(|name| candidate.name == name)
   124	        && (candidate.columns.len() == read.keys.len()
   125	            || named && table.total_unique(candidate) && candidate.columns.len() < read.keys.len())

   129	fn consumer(value: &mut Expr, tables: &BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>, functions: &BTreeMap<String, FunctionDecl>, required: &BTreeSet<String>) {

   132	            choose_consumer(name, args, tables, required);
   133	        }
   134	        Expr::Exists { read, .. } => choose(read, tables, required),
   135	        _ => {},
   136	    }
   137	}

     1	//! Wave 12 language parity on the web-memory and SQLite device stores.
     2	mod common;
     3	use common::{Host, Memory, Sqlite, compile, id, snapshot_pages, stream_page};
     4	use serde_json::json;
     5	use snapback4_core::{Context, Value, WriteState};
     6	
     7	const SOURCE: &str = r#"
     8	table entries:
     9	  category: enum('coffee-shop', 'book_store', 'museum')
    10	  contact: text 3..254 format email
    11	  amount: decimal 2
    12	  search category
    13	  public 'wave 12 device fixture'
    14	  insert <- allow
    15	  sync public
    16	view total: count entries
    17	mutation add(id: id, category: enum('coffee-shop', 'book_store', 'museum'), contact: text 3..254, amount: decimal 2):
    18	  return insert entries { id, category, contact, amount }
    19	query totalEntries():
    20	  return total
    21	query search(q: text <=40, c: cursor ?):
    22	  return entries matching q first 20 by .id after c
    23	query extrema():
    24	  n = count(entries)
    25	  require n > 0 else EMPTY
    26	  return { low: min(entries, .amount), high: max(entries, .amount), sum: sum(entries, .amount) }
    27	query rounded(value: decimal 2):
    28	  return [divide(value, 2, 'half-even'), divide(value, 2, 'half-away-from-zero')]
    29	query average(total: int, count: int):
    30	  return divide(total, count, 2, 'half-even')
    31	query email(value: text 3..254 format email):
    32	  return value
    33	"#;
    34	
    35	fn parity<H: Host>(host: &mut H) {
    36	    let (mut server, backend) = compile(SOURCE);
    37	    server.seed([
    38	        ("entries".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
    39	            ("id", id("a")), ("category", Value::String("coffee-shop".into())),
    40	            ("contact", Value::String("a@example.com".into())), ("amount", Value::Int(5)),
    41	        ])),
    42	        ("entries".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
    43	            ("id", id("b")), ("category", Value::String("book_store".into())),
    44	            ("contact", Value::String("b@example.com".into())), ("amount", Value::Int(-5)),
    45	        ])),
    46	    ]).unwrap();
    47	    let mut device = host.open("alice", Some(backend)).unwrap();
    48	    let pages = snapshot_pages(&mut server, "alice", 100);
    49	    for (index, page) in pages.iter().enumerate() { device.apply(page, index == 0).unwrap(); }
    50	
    51	    assert_eq!(device.query("totalEntries", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(2));
    52	    assert_eq!(device.query("extrema", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data,
    53	        snapback4_core::value::object([("low", Value::Int(-5)), ("high", Value::Int(5)), ("sum", Value::Int(0))]));
    54	    assert_eq!(device.query("rounded", "alice", &json!({"value":5}), 10).unwrap().data,
    55	        Value::Array(vec![Value::Int(2), Value::Int(3)]));
    56	    assert_eq!(device.query("average", "alice", &json!({"total":101,"count":2}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(5050));
    57	    assert_eq!(device.query("average", "alice", &json!({"total":-1,"count":8}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(-12));
    58	    assert_eq!(device.query("email", "alice", &json!({"value":"a@localhost"}), 10).unwrap().data,
    59	        Value::String("a@localhost".into()));
    60	    assert_eq!(device.query("email", "alice", &json!({"value":"a..b@example.com"}), 10).unwrap_err().code, "E_INPUT");
    61	    assert_eq!(device.predict("add", "alice", &json!({
    62	        "id":"bad", "category":"museum", "contact":"a..b@example.com", "amount":0
    63	    }), 10, vec![], 1).unwrap_err().code(), "E_CONSTRAINT", "{}", host.label());
    64	    let hits = device.query("search", "alice", &json!({"q":"cof","c":null}), 10).unwrap();
    65	    assert_eq!(hits.data.as_array().unwrap().len(), 1);
    66	    assert_eq!(hits.data.as_array().unwrap()[0].field("id").unwrap(), &id("a"));
    67	
    68	    let watermark = pages.last().unwrap().watermark;
    69	    let result = server.mutate("add", None, Context::new("alice", [
    70	        ("id".into(), id("c")), ("category".into(), Value::String("museum".into())),
    71	        ("contact".into(), Value::String("c@example.com".into())), ("amount".into(), Value::Int(9)),
    72	    ].into_iter().collect())).unwrap();
    73	    assert!(matches!(result.state, WriteState::Sent { .. }), "{result:?}");
    74	    device.apply(&stream_page(&mut server, "alice", watermark), false).unwrap();
    75	    assert_eq!(device.query("totalEntries", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(3));
    76	    host.close("alice", device);
    77	    let mut reopened = host.open("alice", None).unwrap();
    78	    assert_eq!(reopened.query("totalEntries", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(3),
    79	        "the compiler-owned global discriminator survives store reopen");
    80	    host.close("alice", reopened);
    81	}
    82	
    83	#[test]
    84	fn wave12_semantics_match_on_both_device_stores() {
    85	    parity(&mut Memory::new());
    86	    parity(&mut Sqlite::new("wave12-language"));
    87	}
